# Chapter 1 — comparative apparatus

**Release candidate — final gate pending**

A, B and S are the exact supplied Adzom, Tharpaling and Sichuan transcripts. Their differences are not automatically verified readings of the corresponding print. The chosen reading is Adzom-based; every retained or adopted choice below is a bounded v1 decision, not a reconstructed original.

[Reading](reading.md) · [Coverage](COVERAGE.md) · [Full comparison-scan records](comparison-scans.md) · [Complete structured apparatus](apparatus.json)

The separate [273-block Wikisource apparatus](../reviews/chapter-01/wikisource.md) preserves its exact related Wylie transcription and attribution. It is not an additional independent print witness. Its complete structured data is also included in apparatus.json.

## Accepted scan interventions and source layers

All 88 baseline records remain. Original and adopted strings, smaller annotations, and uncertainty-only records are distinct. Read the stated confidence and locator; a retained annotation can have unresolved printed provenance.

<a id="a2000-c01-c01"></a>
### A2000-C01-C01

Anchors: U00039.

**Supplied text:**
```text
སྟོད་དང་ལྡན་པ་དབུས་མའི་གནས། །
```

**Adopted reading or recorded state:**
```text
སྟོང་དང་ལྡན་པ་དབུས་མའི་གནས། །
```

**Reason:** Final ང is visible in the scan; cleaned e-text has ད. Wikisource stong corroborates the scan but does not govern the correction.

**Locator:** PDF4; BDRC6; Main row 3 right edge begins སྟོང་དང་ལྡན་; row 4 left continues པ་དབུས་མའི་གནས།

**Confidence and limits:** high

**Evidence:** [adzom-004-stong-full.png](../evidence/chapter-01/adzom-004-stong-full.png), [adzom-004-left.png](../evidence/chapter-01/adzom-004-left.png)

<a id="scan-ch1-00542"></a>
### SCAN-CH1-00542

Anchors: U00542.

**Supplied text:**
```text
བཀོད་པ་ལྔ་ཡིས་ཡོངས་སུ་སྨྲས། །
```

**Adopted reading or recorded state:**
```text
བཀོད་པ་ལྔ་ཡིས་ཡོངས་སུ་སྤྲས། །
```

**Reason:** The clause begins at the right end of p23 line 6. The continuation at the left of p24 line 1 clearly reads སུ་སྤྲས།. The scanned stack supports spras, whereas the extracted e-text has smras. Adopt spras as a scan-supported transcription correction and retain the original e-text reading in the apparatus.

**Locator:** {"pdf": "editions/adzom-2000/sgra-thal-gyur.pdf", "pages": [23, 24], "bdrc_images": [25, 26], "image_group": "I1KG11710", "location": "p23 line 6 right end, continuing p24 line 1 left start", "inspected_pixel_rectangles": {"p23-line6-end.png": [3800, 860, 5240, 1080], "p24-top-left.png": [730, 250, 1250, 450]}}

**Confidence and limits:** {"reading": "High", "remaining_uncertainty": "No material uncertainty in the inspected spras reading; this does not verify other U00542 witnesses."}

**Evidence:** [check-p23-line6-end.png](../evidence/chapter-01/check-p23-line6-end.png), [check-p24-top-left.png](../evidence/chapter-01/check-p24-top-left.png)

<a id="scan-ch1-01067"></a>
### SCAN-CH1-01067

Anchors: U01067.

**Supplied text:**
```text
བརྒྱ་ཡང་བྱུང་ཐམས་ཅད་ཚེ་གཅིག་འབྲས་བུ་ཐོབ། །
```

**Adopted reading or recorded state:**
```text
ཐམས་ཅད་ཚེ་གཅིག་འབྲས་བུ་ཐོབ། །
```

**Separate annotation:**
```text
བརྒྱ་ཡང་བྱུང་
```

**Annotation status:** printed_separate_displaced; high for the stated local observation; no claim about other loci

**Separately recorded scan wording (not the unchanged transcript quotation):**
```text
བརྒྱ་ཡང་བྱུང
```

```text
U00029: ཐུན་མོང་གི་གླེང་གཞི་བཀོད་པ། / U00048: རྒྱུད་འབྱུང་གླེང་གཞི་དང་པོ་ལ། ། / U00049: ལྔ་པོ་རྣམས་ཀྱི་ས་བོན་འཛིན། །
```

**Adopted reading or recorded state:**
```text
Existing wording retained; the specifically linked heading-final, prefix and medial glyph questions remain unresolved.
```

**Reason:** The preserved full-page review and a targeted component reinspection do not resolve U00029 heading-final segmentation, U00048 prefix/letter allocation, or the rnams-to-dzin middle of U00049. Their proposed alternative readings are not adopted, and no omission is inferred. This records exact local uncertainty without replacing or restarting the surviving main lexical pass. The separate U00045 extra-dot proposal is disposed by retaining its existing spelling; the supplemental gcig literal label is rejected, not a correction.

```text
བརྒྱུད་གསུམ་⟦Q16⟧ཐོག་མ⟦Q17⟧ ⟦Q18⟧
```

**Confidence:** Partial source reading: medium for first-clause fragments; stronger second-clause correspondence from the saved reader and coordinator. Remaining glyphs and inter-clause signs unresolved.

**Remaining limits:** Q16 is the first-clause run after brgyud gsum and before thog; Q17 is its ending after thog ma; Q18 is the inter-clause sign interval. D denotes one detached pair of uprights, so D D records two spatially separated pairs without assigning ownership from spacing. All bracketed material is editorial, not printed lettering. The earlier full provisional caption is superseded, not retained as an alternate source reading.

## The 183 explicit release choices

All 359 exact transcript differences are represented in these loci. Offsets are zero-based half-open Unicode-character positions in the unmodified full source files. The source quotations below are not changed to match the corrected reading. A quotation containing trailing spaces is rendered as a JSON string so its boundary whitespace stays explicit and round-trippable.

<a id="l1-0001"></a>
### L1-0001

Anchors: U00001, U00002, U00003, U00004, U00005, U00006. Exact differences: C1-0001, C1-0002, C1-0003, C1-0004, C1-0005, C1-0006.

**V1 disposition:** retain_with_explicit_uncertainty

**Reason:** Retain the source-specific Adzom opening, title and invocation scaffold, including the title material not present in the shorter B/S transcript openings. The divergent foreign-script stacks and the invocation are not regularized to either comparison transcript or to reconstructed Sanskrit. Existing title uncertainties remain visible; v1 does not certify those letters.

**Current adopted anchor text:**
```text
{"U00001": "༅།", "U00002": "རཏྞཱཀཱརཤབྡམཧཱཔྲསཾགཏནྟྲནཱམབིཧརཏིསྨ།།", "U00003": "༄༅།", "U00004": " རིན་པོ་ཆེ་འབྱུང་བར་བྱེད་པ་སྒྲ་ཐལ་འགྱུར་ཆེན་པོའི་རྒྱུད་ཅེས་བྱ་བ་བཞུགས།", "U00005": "གཾགརྦམཏགཱ རྒྱ་གར་སྐད་དུ།", "U00006": " རཏྣ་ཀ་ར་ཤབྡ་མ་ཧཱ་པྲ་སཾ་ག་ཏནྟྲ་ནཱ་མ།"}
```

**A [0, 170]:**
```text
༅།རཏྞཱཀཱརཤབྡམཧཱཔྲསཾགཏནྟྲནཱམབིཧརཏིསྨ།།༄༅། རིན་པོ་ཆེ་འབྱུང་བར་བྱེད་པ་སྒྲ་ཐལ་འགྱུར་ཆེན་པོའི་རྒྱུད་ཅེས་བྱ་བ་བཞུགས།གཾགརྦམཏགཱ རྒྱ་གར་སྐད་དུ། རཏྣ་ཀ་ར་ཤབྡ་མ་ཧཱ་པྲ་སཾ་ག་ཏནྟྲ་ནཱ་མ།
```

<a id="l1-0002"></a>
### L1-0002

Anchors: U00010, U00011, U00012. Exact differences: C1-0007, C1-0008, C1-0009, C1-0010.

**V1 disposition:** retain_base

**Reason:** Retain Adzom thos in U00012 rather than substituting the B/S bstan reading. Keep U00011 as a structural heading, not a newly added verse. The B transcript lacks that heading and S changes its boundary presentation; those facts do not authorize deleting the source heading or merging it into the root. Caption S08 remains separate and provisional.

```text
{"U00010": " དཔལ་ཀུན་ཏུ་བཟང་པོ་ལ་ཕྱག་འཚལ་ལོ། །", "U00011": "ཐུན་མོང་མ་ཡིན་པའི་གླེང་གཞི་བཀོད་པ།", "U00012": "འདི་སྐད་བདག་གིས་ཐོས་པའི་དུས་གཅིག་ན། །"}
```

### L1-0183

Anchors: U02635. Exact differences: C1-0357, C1-0358, C1-0359.

**V1 disposition:** retain_with_explicit_uncertainty

**Reason:** Retain U02635 as the Chapter 1 colophon with its exact current rtsa double-tsheg scaffold and punctuation qualified. Do not import the B foreign-script ending or S extra terminal marks. Preserve the separate unread S09 inscription with its own evidence and no conjectural transcription; the known chapter boundary is not a claim that every closing sign is resolved.
